______ ERROR at setup of test_url_server_registers_normal_mcp_tools[json] ______

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_url_server_registers_normal_mcp_tools[json]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10ba71be0>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

        May be overridden.

        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)
>       self.socket.bind(self.server_address)
E       PermissionError: [Errno 1] Operation not permitted

/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/socketserver.py:478: PermissionError
_ ERROR at setup of test_protocol_headers_cannot_be_shadowed_by_custom_header_case[json] _

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_protocol_headers_cannot_be_shadowed_by_custom_header_case[json]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/socketserver.py:457: in __init__
    self.server_bind()
/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/http/server.py:148: in server_bind
    socketserver.TCPServer.server_bind(self)
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10b9ab240>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)
>       self.socket.bind(self.server_address)
E       PermissionError: [Errno 1] Operation not permitted

/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/socketserver.py:478: PermissionError
____ ERROR at setup of test_unsupported_protocol_is_skipped[sse-2099-01-01] ____

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_unsupported_protocol_is_skipped[sse-2099-01-01]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10bc15a90>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)
>       self.socket.bind(self.server_address)

/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/socketserver.py:478: PermissionError
_ ERROR at setup of test_bad_url_does_not_break_other_servers[json-http://127.0.0.1:70000/mcp] _

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_bad_url_does_not_break_other_servers[json-http://127.0.0.1:70000/mcp]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10b9d8710>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/socketserver.py:478: PermissionError
_ ERROR at setup of test_bad_url_does_not_break_other_servers[sse-http://127.0.0.1:70000/mcp] _

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_bad_url_does_not_break_other_servers[sse-http://127.0.0.1:70000/mcp]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10bbfc870>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/socketserver.py:478: PermissionError
_____ ERROR at setup of test_failed_calls_are_not_replayed[json-wrong_id] ______

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_failed_calls_are_not_replayed[json-wrong_id]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass

                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10bce2750>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_failed_calls_are_not_replayed[sse-wrong_id]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]

                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10bcc7460>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):

                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))

                else:

                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)

                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10bcb5080>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10be62c10>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10be90a60>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)
>       self.socket.bind(self.server_address)
E       PermissionError: [Errno 1] Operation not permitted

/Library/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/3.14/lib/python3.14/socketserver.py:478: PermissionError
____ ERROR at setup of test_malformed_handshake_is_skipped[json-override2] _____

request = <SubRequest 'server' for <Function test_malformed_handshake_is_skipped[json-override2]>>

    @pytest.fixture(params=["json", "sse"])
    def server(request):
        calls, wire_headers = [], []
        settings = {"version": "2025-06-18", "session": "session-one", "mode": "normal"}
        response_type = request.param
    
        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    
            def handle_one_request(self):
                try:
                    super().handle_one_request()
                except (ConnectionResetError, BrokenPipeError):
                    self.close_connection = True  # the client may finish before the SSE connection does
    
            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                calls.append((request, dict(self.headers)))
                wire_headers.append(self.headers)
                method = request["method"]
                if method == "notifications/initialized":
                    self.send_response(202)
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                    self.end_headers()
                    return
                mode = settings["mode"] if method == "tools/call" else "normal"
                if method == "initialize":
                    result = {"protocolVersion": settings["version"], "capabilities": {"tools": {}}}
                elif method == "tools/list":
                    result = {"tools": [{"name": "echo", "description": "Echo text", "inputSchema": {"type": "object"}}]}
                else:
                    result = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": request["params"]["arguments"]["text"]}]}
                if mode == "late_error":
                    entered, release = settings["gate"]
                    entered.set()
                    release.wait(5)
                reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "result": result}
                if mode == "rpc_error":
                    reply = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": request["id"], "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "bad text"}}
                elif mode == "wrong_id":
                    reply["id"] += 100
                if "reply_override" in settings:
                    reply.update(settings["reply_override"])
                body = json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
                if mode == "framed":
                    ending = settings["ending"]
                    body = b"\xef\xbb\xbf" + ending.join(
                        b"data: " + line for line in json.dumps(reply, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode().split(b"\n")
                    ) + ending * 2
                elif response_type == "sse":
                    body = (b': keepalive\n\nevent: message\ndata: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/progress"}\n\n'
                            + b"data: " + body + b"\n\n")
                if mode == "oversize":
                    body = b" " * (4 * 1024 * 1024) + body
                if mode == "invalid_json":
                    body = b"data: {\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b"{"
                self.send_response(503 if mode == "late_error" else 404 if mode == "expired" else 302 if mode == "redirect" else 200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json" if response_type == "json" else "text/event-stream")
                if mode == "redirect":
                    self.send_header("Location", f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}/other")
                if mode == "drip":
                    prefix = b": keepalive\n\n" if response_type == "sse" else b" "
                    body = prefix * 20 + body
                if settings.get("chunked"):
                    self.send_header("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
                else:
                    self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body) + (mode == "truncated")))
                if mode == "truncated":
                    self.send_header("Connection", "close")
                    self.close_connection = True
                if method == "initialize" and settings["session"] is not None:
                    self.send_header("Mcp-Session-Id", settings["session"])
                self.end_headers()
                try:
                    if mode == "drip":
                        for _ in range(20):
                            self.wfile.write(prefix)
                            self.wfile.flush()
                            time.sleep(0.03)
                        body = body[len(prefix) * 20:]
                    if settings.get("chunked"):
                        size = 1 if mode == "framed" else 65536
                        for offset in range(0, len(body), size):
                            chunk = body[offset:offset + size]
                            self.wfile.write(f"{len(chunk):x}\r\n".encode() + chunk + b"\r\n")
                        self.wfile.write(b"0\r\n\r\n")
                    else:
                        self.wfile.write(body)
                except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
                    pass
    
            def do_DELETE(self):
                calls.append(({"method": "DELETE"}, dict(self.headers)))
                self.send_response(settings.get("delete_status", 200))
                self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
                self.end_headers()
    
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass
    
>       httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer object at 0x10be91780>

    def server_bind(self):
        """Called by constructor to bind the socket.
    
        May be overridden.
    
        """
        if self.allow_reuse_address and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEADDR"):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        # Since Linux 6.12.9, SO_REUSEPORT is not allowed
        # on other address families than AF_INET/AF_INET6.
        if (
            self.allow_reuse_port and hasattr(socket, "SO_REUSEPORT")
            and self.address_family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6)
        ):
            self.socket.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEPORT, 1)
